```json
  "scope": "除PRO商业授权门槛及用户既有明确品牌、深色、JWT、自有探针/仅外部Komari、三私有仓要求外，按妙妙屋官方公开行为和固定版本验证结果对齐。未公开内部机制记录为待核对，不用自行发明架构或状态机替代。",

    {
      "name": "ASWired",

      "name": "ASWired-Server",
      "role": "主控后端",
      "visibility": "private"
    },
    {
      "name": "ASWired-Agent",
      "role": "Agent同进程embedded核心、自有主机采集、节点运维及同仓家用测速端",
      "visibility": "private"
    }
  ],
  "user_overrides": [
    {
      "id": "aswired_brand",
      "instruction": "名称ASWired，采用用户指定图标；不复制上游品牌身份。"
    },
    {
      "id": "dark_only",
      "instruction": "只支持深色，不提供白天模式、系统跟随或个人切换；CSS须维持该产品约束。"
    },
    {
      "id": "external_probe_komari_only",
      "instruction": "仅外部第三方探针接入限定Komari1.2.5-fix2；ASWired自有采集、内置/独立域名/Worker展示、API/WS历史与密码/Passkey登录回接全部保留。独立部署自有展示端不是第三方探针供应商。"
    },
    {
      "id": "three_private_repositories",
      "instruction": "网站、主控后端、子Agent分别独立仓库，保持私有，用户明确确认后才改public。"
    },
    {
      "id": "user_auth_jwt",
      "instruction": "网站与自有探针用户认证强制采用JWT；MM-Authorization和过期重登按官方用户页对齐；其余时效、算法、刷新与回接机制待公开事实/固定版本核对。"
    },
    {
      "id": "no_pro_license_gate",
      "instruction": "所有PRO功能纳入ASWired统一发行，不设付费墙、商业许可证或授权配额；Agent Token、主控公钥、securechan、JWT及联邦等技术鉴权全部保留。"
    }
  ],
  "phases": [
    {
      "id": "P0",
      "name": "契约基础",
      "exit": "三仓边界、官方公开契约/固定版本样本与未知项登记；不先固定上游未公开的命令/持久化状态机。"
    },
    {
      "id": "P1",
      "name": "运行时配置身份",
      "exit": "Token/主控公钥/securechan连接、用户JWT、external/同进程embedded及基础原生采集按固定版本核对。"
    },
    {
      "id": "P2",
      "name": "用户管控计量",
      "exit": "动态用户、速度/连接/IP上限、自动限速、权益与账本联动。"
    },
    {
      "id": "P3",
      "name": "节点运维网络证书",
      "exit": "服务、端口、地址、路由转发、证书及运维支持矩阵；自有采集器部署完善、内置/独立站点与Worker部署、域名和登录客户端配置。"
    },
    {
      "id": "P4",
      "name": "测速联邦",
      "exit": "主控/家用测速、来源回退和受限跨主控共享。"
    },
    {
      "id": "P5",
      "name": "全业务网站集成",
      "exit": "全部成员/管理业务、订阅模板、TG/MCP、外观；自有探针完整HTTP/WS、历史展示、密码/Passkey登录回接及Komari双来源整合。"
    },
    {
      "id": "P6",
      "name": "发布恢复全矩阵验收",
      "exit": "全部功能与PRO、承诺平台/协议和实际升级/恢复行为完成固定版本对照；未知关键项未核实不能标完整对齐。"
    }
  ],
  "implementation_note": "当前仅设计和前端原型；168条implementation_status均为not_implemented。实施阶段不是删减范围。验收条件是待执行的公开行为/固定版本对照用例，不证明已知上游内部机制。",
  "license_boundary": "不实现PRO付费墙/商业许可证门槛，不复用或绕过上游商业授权；保留全部技术消息鉴权。各依赖实现及分发许可仍按实际来源核实。",
  "open_evidence_gate": "私有协议、离线行为、升级恢复、JWT参数及内部数据结构未知时明确登记待核对；不得将自创状态机或新部署架构当作已对齐事实。",
  "status_legend": {
    "committed": "官方公开行为的完整范围目标，需固定版本验证",
    "user_override": "用户已明确要求的品牌/深色/JWT/外部探针/免PRO许可差异",
    "functional_equivalent_with_explicit_boundary": "公开能力保留，私有格式及互操作须固定版本/合法样本验证",
    "additional_detail": "原功能的细化用例，不增加原始功能计数",
    "confirmed_documented": "官方文档说明，未运行验证",
    "confirmed_release_notes": "公开发布说明，未运行验证",
    "public_behavior_internal_mechanism_pending": "公开能力有依据，具体内部实现待核对",
    "aswired_distribution_decision": "用户决定ASWired不设商业付费墙",
    "upstream_removed_in_target_prerelease": "该历史能力在目标预发布已移除，保留追溯但不重建替代市场"
  },
  "probe_boundary": "ASWired自有采集、内置/独立域名/Worker展示与密码/Passkey回接完整保留；仅外部第三方来源限制Komari1.2.5-fix2。JWT由用户明确要求；回接具体协议按公开实现/固定版本核对。Komari凭据和只读密钥不是用户登录。",
  "phase_interpretation": "单行阶段标记主工作包起点，不表示整行全部能力同时完成。原生采集/注册绑定及基础身份在P1，部署运维在P3，完整历史展示、跨域回接与Komari整合在P5；所有行最终统一P6验收。",
  "user_authentication": {
    "format": "JWT",
    "requirement_source": "explicit_user_requirement",
    "public_reference_behavior": {
      "header": "MM-Authorization",
      "expiry": "Token过期后重新登录",
      "source": "https://miaomiaowux.com/docs/users/"
    },
    "unpublished_parameters": [
      "签名算法/密钥轮换细节",
      "完整claims及验证参数",
      "有效期数值",
      "是否存在刷新令牌及刷新规则",
      "浏览器存储与跨域回接机制",
      "退出/停用的内部失效机制"
    ],
    "upstream_conflict": "官方用户页说明JWT；README的JWT_SECRET配置及未设置时随机Token描述需固定版本核对。ASWired按用户要求强制JWT，原始上游审计事实不改写。",
    "non_user_credentials": "Agent按token/master_public_key/securechan技术身份链；Komari凭据、公开探针读取密钥和订阅凭据不自动转为用户JWT。"
  },
  "architecture_reference": {
    "embedded": "xray-core嵌入Agent同一进程，同进程调用、退出及升级",
    "external": "独立Xray进程，由Agent经gRPC管理",
    "agent_auth": "独立agent_token + master_public_key + securechan，三种连接按官方行为核对",
    "vision": "VisionLimiterHook接入splice限速，无限速保留原始splice；copy-only不是对齐替代",
    "docker": "官方host网络+embedded，包含Nginx与依赖；按公开安装/compose核对",
    "federation": "consumer操作经owner转发到Agent，owner不能读取消费方正文；正文不在owner明文重编译",
    "internal_unknowns": "离线许可、命令账本、升级回滚及内部并发机制未公开；无独立helper/coordinator/observer/sidecar或availability/accounting模式的新增目标承诺"
  }
}
```

早期ASWired业务接口参考草案，不是妙妙屋私有API声明，也不是新增角色、审批或账务流程的实施要求；当前功能以第15份矩阵为准。Agent通信按第13至17份设计核对上游协议，本文件不再定义替代协议。用户JWT采用MM-Authorization，具体格式和有效期以第18份固定版本验证为准。\nservers:\n  - url: https://aswired.example.test\nsecurity:\n  - UserJWT: []\n  - ApiToken: []\npaths:\n  /api/v1/servers:\n    get:\n      operationId: listServers\n      summary: 分页读取本主体有权查看的服务器\n      parameters:\n        - {name: cursor, in: query, schema: {type: string}}\n        - {name: limit, in: query, schema: {type: integer, minimum: 1, maximum: 100, default: 20}}\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: 列表不包含密钥\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                properties:\n                  data: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Server'}}\n                  nextCursor: {type: [string, 'null']}\n        '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/Failure'}\n    post:\n      operationId: createServer\n      summary: 创建服务器库存，不自动安装 Agent\n      parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey'}]\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required: [name, address, backendKind]\n              properties:\n                name: {type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 100}\n                address: {type: string, maxLength: 253}\n                backendKind: {type: string, enum: [native-agent, 3xui-adapter, external-readonly]}\n      responses:\n        '201':\n          description: 已创建库存\n          content: {application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Server'}}}\n        '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/Failure'}\n  /api/v1/servers/{id}:\n    parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/ResourceId'}]\n    patch:\n      operationId: updateServerInventory\n      summary: 按版本更新库存字段\n      parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/IfMatch'}]\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              minProperties: 1\n              properties:\n                name: {type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 100}\n                region: {type: string, maxLength: 100}\n                monthlyCostMinor: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/UnsignedDecimal'}\n                currency: {type: string, pattern: '^[A-Z]{3}$'}\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: 更新库存成功，不等于运行配置已发布\n          content: {application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Server'}}}\n        '412': {$ref: '#/components/responses/Failure'}\n  /api/v1/carpools/{id}/seat-holds:\n    parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/ResourceId'}]\n    post:\n      operationId: reserveCarpoolSeat\n      summary: 在事务内暂留最后席位，过期时间由后端确定\n      parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey'}]\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required: [invitationToken]\n              properties:\n                invitationToken: {type: string, minLength: 16, maxLength: 256, writeOnly: true}\n      responses:\n        '201':\n          description: 暂留成功；调用主体来自会话，不能由请求冒充其他成员\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                required: [id, state, expiresAt]\n                properties:\n                  id: {type: string, format: uuid}\n                  state: {const: RESERVED}\n                  expiresAt: {type: string, format: date-time}\n        '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/Failure'}\n  /api/v1/billing-periods/{id}/allocation-preview:\n    parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/ResourceId'}]\n    post:\n      operationId: previewCostAllocation\n      summary: 按后端费用项和冻结参与者试算，不写入财务分录\n      requestBody:\n        required: true\n        content:\n          application/json:\n            schema:\n              type: object\n              additionalProperties: false\n              required: [policy, participantVersion]\n              properties:\n                policy: {type: string, enum: [all-seats, active-members, weighted]}\n                participantVersion: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/UnsignedDecimal'}\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: 报价快照，金额总和等于分摊成本\n          content:\n            application/json:\n              schema:\n                type: object\n                properties:\n                  previewId: {type: string, format: uuid}\n                  totalMinor: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/UnsignedDecimal'}\n                  currency: {type: string}\n                  expiresAt: {type: string, format: date-time}\n                  shares:\n                    type: array\n                    items:\n                      type: object\n                      properties:\n                        subjectId: {type: string, format: uuid}\n                        amountMinor: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/UnsignedDecimal'}\n        '412': {$ref: '#/components/responses/Failure'}\n  /api/v1/subjects/{id}/credential-rotations:\n    parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/ResourceId'}]\n    post:\n      operationId: rotateSubjectCredentials\n      summary: 创建独立凭据轮换任务，逐节点确认撤销旧凭据\n      parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey'}, {$ref: '#/components/parameters/IfMatch'}]\n      responses:\n        '202': {$ref: '#/components/responses/Accepted'}\n        '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/Failure'}\n  /api/v1/operations/{id}:\n    parameters: [{$ref: '#/components/parameters/ResourceId'}]\n    get:\n      operationId: getOperation\n      summary: 查询每个目标的交付结果，不将部分成功显示为全部完成\n      responses:\n        '200':\n          description: 操作投影\n          content: {application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Operation'}}}\n        '404': {$ref: '#/components/responses/Failure'}\ncomponents:\n  securitySchemes:\n    UserJWT: {type: apiKey, in: header, name: MM-Authorization, description: 携带签名JWT；apiKey仅用于描述自定义HTTP头，具体前缀、算法和有效期待固定版本验证，不表示采用随机API密钥}\n    ApiToken: {type: http, scheme: bearer, description: 业务API Token示例；与登录JWT和节点Token分离，确切格式与权限按上游固定版本核对}\n  parameters:\n    ResourceId: {name: id, in: path, required: true, schema: {type: string, format: uuid}}\n    IfMatch: {name: If-Match, in: header, required: true, schema: {type: string}, description: 资源读取返回的ETag；缺失返回428，不匹配412}\n    IdempotencyKey: {name: Idempotency-Key, in: header, required: true, schema: {type: string, minLength: 16, maxLength: 128}, description: 同主体同路径同请求重放返回原结果，冲突请求体返回409}\n  schemas:\n    UnsignedDecimal: {type: string, pattern: '^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$', description: 金额、字节、版本以十进制字符串跨JSON传递}\n    Server:\n      type: object\n      required: [id, name, version, backendKind]\n      properties:\n        id: {type: string, format: uuid}\n        name: {type: string}\n        version: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/UnsignedDecimal'}\n        backendKind: {type: string, enum: [native-agent, 3xui-adapter, external-readonly]}\n        observedAt: {type: [string, 'null'], format: date-time}\n        freshness: {type: string, enum: [FRESH, STALE, UNKNOWN]}\n    Operation:\n      type: object\n      required: [id, state, targets]\n      properties:\n        id: {type: string, format: uuid}\n        state: {type: string, enum: [QUEUED, RUNNING, PARTIAL, SUCCEEDED, FAILED, CANCELLED]}\n        requiredTargetsSatisfied: {type: boolean}\n        targets:\n          type: array\n          items:\n            type: object\n            required: [nodeId, state]\n            properties:\n              nodeId: {type: string, format: uuid}\n              state: {type: string, enum: [QUEUED, NODE_PENDING, PREPARED, APPLIED, VERIFIED, FAILED]}\n              errorCode: {type: [string, 'null']}\n              retryable: {type: boolean}\n    Failure:\n      type: object\n      required: [error, meta]\n      properties:\n        error:\n          type: object\n          required: [code, message, retryable]\n          properties:\n            code: {type: string}\n            message: {type: string}\n            retryable: {type: boolean}\n        meta:\n          type: object\n          properties:\n            requestId: {type: string}\n  responses:\n    Failure:\n      description: 结构化错误，敏感资源信息不泄露\n      content: {application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Failure'}}}\n    Accepted:\n      description: 已持久化任务，尚未声明节点生效\n      content: {application/json: {schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Operation'}}}\n"
